ParlaMint-AT_2010-10-21-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00081_d8e3651	Mrs Bundesminister, please.
ParlaMint-AT_2010-10-21-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00081_d8e3666	Ladies and gentlemen! I wish you a good morning. – Madam President, I am very grateful to you for this because we see that there is still a need for clarification at European level as far as the liberalisation of the rail market is concerned. Not only has Austria, as regards implementation, been admonished, but a total of 24 EU Member States have been members of the 27 Member States. Three points in the implementation were questioned by the EU. The first point concerns the sanctions of the regulatory bodies. We have now been able to convince the EU Commission or make it credible that we have dealt with this point with autonomy within the framework of the Rail Control Commission, and after that the warning has been withdrawn. The second point that has been criticised concerns the conditions of network allocation. The aim was to introduce a bonus malus system in terms of operating and driving contracts on these routes. This claim has already been implemented. With 1. January 2011 will be so far that we also have such a bonus malus system in Austria. A bonus is then available when, for example, light, modern and quiet vehicles are driven, and a malus when it comes to delays. And the third point that was criticized concerns the award of the tracks, the route assignments. We are still in negotiations with the European Union.

I should just like to point out that in 2009 we were promoting more than 14 000 renovations and investment in scale within 12 weeks. have set in motion some €500 million. So it was also a full success from sustainability. Now to your question why we have used and handled a cash grant and no credit subsidies, no interest subsidy on the loans. This has the following background: We also conducted surveys with the implementing organisations, which were essentially the construction savings banks and, on the other hand, the municipal credit company. Reference is made, and there we have seen that about 65 percent of those concerned use their own savings for the project, especially in the case of thermal renovation. That is to say, the wish of those concerned is to act accordingly with their own savings and additional investments and subsidies from the State. The other question, whether they would do so when crediting, has shown that much less would be used. That means in the clear text: From our point of view, a promoter could take a loan with these funds, with the aid, and on the other hand could take the grant as he has taken it so far. A second point, which we have seen as a problem, was the link between the promotions of the countries. There we have first and foremost annuity subsidies, partly already in progress, and we have had an additive effect there. With this additive effect, it was better to bring this additional element from the content side than to make complicated backhandlings and similarities. Thirdly, as far as interest subsidies are concerned, one of our considerations was that this entails a considerable administrative burden. When we deal with this, it requires permanent care over years, and that is why we thought it was better – also seen from the administration – to act in this way. My proposal is: We look at all the models and can evaluate the additive in the sum. If we have a choice for the consumer, for the advertiser, then, I would say, that's the best. And the very best thing about this is if we get the money back in the framework of the budgetary negotiations for these measures. That's the most important thing. Otherwise, we can talk about models.

Then: Only what you can see and understand can you live. This is probably the second best way, but still better than to leave the fragmentation. We will come to a conclusion in the next few weeks. I think that's my legitimate hope.

ParlaMint-AT_2010-10-21-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00081_d8e10952	Mr President. Minister! High house! The KünstlerInnen Social Security Structure Act, which we decide today, is another step towards social security for artists in our country. We don't see this from the SPÖ, as my colleague, my colleague, has explained before me, that he wants to look at the artists over his shoulders, but we consider it essential to ensure the flexible situations of the lives of artists in a flexible way in our social system. A programme item has been set in the 2008 government programme. As a result, there was an interministerial working group, which involved a total of nine ministries. A study on the social situation has also led to proposals being put on the table. There was an intensive dialogue and exchange with stakeholders and also a national conference on the social situation of the creatives in our country. The essential points of this Social Insurance Act are the creation of a Centre of Competence for Artists. Especially because of the flexible situation of life or the fact that artists have changing working conditions – from self-employed work to self-employed work – it has always been difficult for those concerned to obtain correct information. A service agency will now be set up to cover all areas of unemployment insurance and the Künstler-Socialversicherungsfonds. In other words, the artists no longer have to run from place to place, but this is taken from them within the scope of the service station. This is a special relief and recognition of this particular situation of life and a simplification for artists in Austria. This is a very important support, because artists and cultural workers in our country are being promoted. Furthermore, there is the possibility of retiring the work of artistic activity in order to be able to obtain benefits from unemployment insurance. Overall, this law is a very important contribution to the social security of artists in our country.

Point Report of the Committee on Labour and Social Affairs on the Government Template (865d.b._Other Organiser Agreement between the Republic of Austria and Montenegro on social security (902 d.a.)
ParlaMint-AT_2010-10-21-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00081_d8e12194	We're now on the 4th. Agenda item. Mr Karlsböck was the first to speak. Please, my colleague.
ParlaMint-AT_2010-10-21-024-XXIV-NRSITZ-00081_d8e12219	Mr President. Minister! Ladies and gentlemen! This point in today's agenda is likely to be a ‘runner’. As the only speaker of my group, I would therefore like to make it brief, but a few words should already be said about this. In principle, Montenegro should be brought closer to a European level, not only in football, as now in the European Championship qualification, where Montenegro was able to reach a final decision against England. All efforts in this area to bring Montenegro closer to the European Union should be welcomed. Following the division of the former Federal Republic of Yugoslavia, the agreement between Austria and the Federal Republic of Yugoslavia was applied pragmatically. Montenegro, however, was interested in replacing this agreement with a new one, also to emphasize its own country. The present new agreement preserves the current protection in the field of health, accident and pension insurance and unemployment insurance with essentially the same material content, but at the same time formally adapts it to the other social security agreements concluded by Austria in recent years. The current agreement between the Republic of Austria and Montenegro on social security promotes Montenegro's own country and provides important impetus for a more comprehensive data protection system. Other projects, such as the Twinning project, also promote development in this area. The Twinning project aims to strengthen Montenegro's capacity to protect personal data and to implement data protection legislation. A project component deals with the further harmonisation of data protection legislation, the EU acquis. Another aim is to assist the recently established independent data protection authority with targeted training in building up the activity of the Authority. Trainings for data protection officers in ministries, other relevant institutions as well as in the private sector are also part of this project. This is a positive development so that we can agree.

I therefore think this is necessary, right and good, because on the one hand it is clear that you are also more self-employed with your own income, and because an employment with an income that you can also expect is a guarantee in old age. What I think is right and what I believe must be taken into account, is the fact that there has been a very strong increase in the part-time rate. When I imagine that the number of women working part-time has almost doubled in the last 15 years – in 1995 it was 367,000 women and in 2009 it is 732 000 women – I think that must wake us up. There is, of course, the fact that many women also want to do so because it is a way to better reconcile work and private life. I still prefer this to be women who leave the job entirely. Kitzmüller.]] However, ladies and gentlemen, what is behind it is the fact that many women, even though they want to work full-time, do not get full-time employment because they are not offered on the labour market or because they do not have the appropriate framework conditions. I therefore believe that this report on women is not only a very good statistical document, it also gives us the opportunity to act precisely and where it is necessary to take action. I believe, in particular in the field of part-time employment, that we should focus on the possibility of providing child care facilities/child training facilities throughout Austria, but that measures should continue to be taken to involve fathers a little more in the responsibility of family responsibilities. That too, as I believe, is very important. What seems also important to me is the fact that if you are part-time employed in a farm, you have the opportunity to switch to full-time employment more easily in the operation. In the daily practice of foreign policy, we can put in place many small measures to ensure access for women to information, health care, infrastructure, water, energy, transport, education, development funds. At UN level, something has happened. There is a new unit, UN Women, which will be responsible for the implementation of Resolution 1325. The former Chilean President Bachelet has taken the lead in this unity. We also have a United Nations Special Representative for Sexual Violence against Women, and it will also be a strong voice on this issue. However, karenz times are calculated in very few collective contracts. It takes longer for workers to be classified into a higher salary class. However, we of the FPÖ believe that women and men who care about their children must not be punished. That is what I am fighting for, and I have done so at the European level. The European Union says there must be a genetic engineering agriculture, there must be a so-called co-existence between organic, conventional and GMO agriculture. In order not to do so, we have established the GMO varieties approved at European level in Austria with a ban on cultivation. In March of last year, it was possible for the Commission – and so it was for the fourth time – to request that these national cultivation bans, which are not only for us, but also for Hungary, for example, be tipped, and one must not receive a simple majority each time, but a qualified majority of 75 percent of the weighted votes. In March 2009, I was able – for the fourth time – to defend the ban on cultivation. This cannot be the common Europe that Europe tells us what we are cultivating in our fields. At the time, I started the initiative “Right of self-determination on the issue of genetic engineering”. I have been supported by some Member States. And at the request in June 2009, many have said, as in the case of Example Germany: You stoked into a weaponst, you won't get through with it! The United Kingdom has said that it is against WTO rules. At any rate, the great political success of this initiative, which was taken with many states that came from a wide range of parties – not only conservative states, but also Holland, for example; the Social Democratic Environment Minister supported me there at the time – was that the new Commission took over after the European elections. Barroso has taken over this Austrian initiative for self-determination in genetic engineering. We are very happy about this, I am pleased about it, and it is now up to the Commission to enforce it legally. EU Health Commissioner Dalli has requested in the Commission that regions can declare themselves free of genetic engineering – Austria is such – and that the whole must also be legalised by amending the EU Release Directive. There are now discussions on this. There has been such a situation in the Council of Agriculture Ministers and in the Council of Environment Ministers. There are still different points of view. The ball is now with the European Commission, it is due to the fact that this legal security is also being taken. Austria will support it because we want to remain free of genetic engineering in the future. This is what our agriculture wants, but that is what our consumers also want. That is the objective of our domestic agricultural policy, and I am still committed to doing so at European level. Thank you very much.

Office note (§95) After being able to reach Natascha Kampusch, CI Kurt Linzer agreed on 15.10.2009 by the Eost Chief State Procuratorate Graz, at which he As well as the prosecutors were willing to build a trust relationship, Natascha declared himself willing to actively support the investigators in case-processing. Kampusch Natascha also handed over to Cl Linzer, among other things, various documents kept in the house Strasshof, Heinestraße 60, which were subsequently followed and which she now kept in her apartment, for inspection or evaluation. These are disks for the Commodore 64, which is owned by Wolfgang Priklopil. On these disks, it was found that although the imgage files on them were listed by the officials who were then caught, the contents of the respective file had not been viewed, but apparently not. Therefore, an evaluation of relevant files has been started. As a result, Kampusch Natascha declared to Cl Linzer that he was willing to talk to Ischtar Akcan at the time with the aim of taking the still existing fear that the second man who was involved in the act could go against her and, on the other hand, by this conversation, the existing contradictions between her information and the information of the lawyer Ischtar Akcan can be clarified. Due to this willingness by Natascha Kampusch, a detailed conversation was held by Colonel Kröll, both with Ischtar Akcan and with her mother Rosa Akcan, as well as the willingness of both persons to talk with Natascha Kampusch with the goals pursued. After both Natascha Kampusch and the lawyer Ischtar Akcan were able to achieve the willingness to talk with the goals, a comparison according to § 163 criminal order is permitted, in particular in the case of different statements, if it is assumed that they can resolve contradictions. Between Natasha Kampusch and Ischtar Akcan was agreed on the 03.12.2009, beginning at 18:30 a.m., in the ho. Federal Criminal Office, agreed as the date of the discussion for the planned project, with both persons being opened up to be able to teach a trustee each. In the course of this official act: Start: 03.12.2009, 18:45. Locality: Federal Criminal Office, Service Area 4336 Officials: Colonel Kröll and Cl Linzer Participants: Kampusch Natasha, Dr. Ganzger, Silveri John (Successor) Akcan Ischtar, Akcan Rosa (Mother) Col. Kröll welcomed the participants, thanked them for their willingness to participate and presented the objectives again, and made it absolutely clear that the different statements between Natascha Kampusch and Ischtar Akcan regarding the number of persons involved in the performance of the act were in no way due to a conscious untruth, but rather because of the “empertured” they may have led to an irritation in the perception of the Tathergang. After that, Kampusch Natascha told the Tathergang she had experienced, explaining that she had first noticed Wolfgang Priklopil, when he was already outside the vehicle, captured her, spent her in the vehicle and on the completely empty loading area. Immediately after that, Wolfgang Priklopil would have entered, closed the sliding door and would have reached the driver's seat over the empty load area and actually got off immediately. She also noticed that Wolfgang Priklopil was driving the vehicle quickly. As a result, she had the impression that Wolfgang Priklopil had driven the vehicle in the area of Lieblgasse-Maculangasse in the circle for the first time, according to her assessment for hours, and had only been driving towards Strasshof. During the entire journey from the abduction site away to the intermediate stop in a forest piece and the drive to the house in Strasshof, she certainly did not notice a second person in the vehicle or would not have entered or left the vehicle during that time. If in fact a second person had been in the vehicle, she would surely have noticed it and told the police about it. Note to the descriptions of Natascha Kampusch: Their descriptions were liquid and also consistent with their shown body language. There was no suspicion from the point of view of the officials present that they were prepared for these descriptions. She spoke freely, did not make any eye contact with the officials, acted securely, and also answered the interim questions asked her by the officials spontaneously. Akcan Ischtar, but also her mother Rosa Akcan, appeared at the beginning of the descriptions of Natascha Kampusch and expressed this by the body language. As a result, this tension broke down and became apparent in solubleness and relief. After the descriptions of Natascha Kampusch, Ischtar Akcan told her about the Tathergang. At the time when she was on the school road and was walking on the foothills of the race track, she had a distance from approx. 20 meters on the opposite side of the road a white boxing car and in the vehicle on the driver's seat a man perceived. No one would have been sitting in the passenger seat at the time. This perception would have been completely by chance and had it not thought at all or caused or caused something of this perception in it. It was only when she was in the height of the vehicle's rearing area that she saw a man whom she later no doubt recognized as Wolfgang Priklopil, who packed a girl that she also recognized on the fencing posters in no doubt as Natascha Kampusch, who could have heard a scream of the girl. Then she heard the blow of a door and still saw that the vehicle was rolling. Then she was afraid of being in a bush at the do. Hiding dog's meadow. The vehicle would have stood for a few minutes and then left. Note: Kampusch Natascha suggested that the course was moving very quickly and it did not have the impression that the vehicle was still standing for a few minutes after it was already in the vehicle, but went away quite quickly. On interviews, Ischtar Akcan claimed that she had no visible contact with the driver cabin from a bush of the dog meadow and Therefore, it was not possible to see if two people were there while driving. At any rate, Ischtar Akcan waited in the bush for the vehicle to leave and only after that came out of the bush. At the time of the occurrence and the continuation of her school route, she could not have seen the vehicle on the track and therefore could not indicate the direction in which the vehicle actually continued in the circular traffic. Note: The entry of the vehicle from the road to the circular traffic and then the continuing journey, the Witness Mathilde Heisinger observed from the balcony of her apartment in Melangasse 3, observing that the vehicle was approaching the circuit at high speed, entering it and continuing the journey on the Melangasse. Akcan Ischtar would have gone further on the track towards Wagramerstraße after coming out of the bush at the dog meadow. At the crossing of Rennbahnweg-Panethgasse, it would have been right at the time when they had wanted to cross this crossroads, therefore the vehicle perceived by it at the place of action on the Panethgasse from the left (seen from its location) had stopped and would then have walked it over the road in front of the stopped vehicle. When she noticed the vehicle there, she got scared again and thought that she too was being abducted. Therefore, it would have walked over the Panethgasse in front of the stopped vehicle. During her perception she also saw that in the vehicle were two men, remembering from the handlebar that he was light-skinned and had a so-called stopper glass. When asked the Colonel Kröll if she saw two men sitting in the vehicle at this location for the first time, Ischtar Akcan suggested that this was the right thing to do. Note: The distance between the crime scene and the bush at the dog meadow to the crossroads Rennbahnweg-Panethgasse is about 350 meters. Therefore, Ischtar must have gone from the bush to the mentioned intersection on the pavement of the race track about 350 meters. The waiting time for the bend into the time for handling the roadway to the Panethgasse, allows the statement that a few minutes had passed until Ischtar Akcan then noticed the vehicle coming from the left again on the Panethgasse. Note from Natascha Kampusch: She stated to Ischtar Akcan that she had gone to school more or less the same way and had seen several white boxing cars again and again. Her father Ludwig too Koch has used for the export of the pastry goods including white boxing cars. She claimed that she would have thought that the kidnapper was an employee of her father who might have been angry with her father, especially since the car was white. She also asked Wolfgang Priklopil during the ride if he was an employee of her father. (These figures are new and have not yet been made by Natascha Kampusch.) After Ischtar Akcan stated that she had first seen two men sitting on the crossing of Rennbahnweg-Panethgasse in the white box car, and because of the information given by Natascha Kampusch, after which she was taken to the empty loading surface in the vehicle, therefore, no further seats were in the vehicle except the driver's and passenger's seat, the Colonel Kröll also explained the following consideration for her perception of the Tathergang: ==References====External links== It takes 20 meters simply by chance the white boxing car and a man sitting at the driver’s seat was without taking further care of this vehicle or the man sitting at the driver’s seat. After her accidental perception, she goes about 20 meters until she is at the same height with the front side of the vehicle, passing by the vehicle and only when she was in the rear of the vehicle, from the opposite side of the road, her perception to the Tathergang. Therefore, it was possible that Wolfgang Priklopil from the passenger seat, observed the approach of Natascha Kampus over the outside mirror on the passenger side, went from the vehicle seat to the empty loading area to the sliding door, opened it, left the vehicle and already outside the vehicle expected to approach the vehicle, captured it and moved it to the vehicle or to the empty loading area, then closed the sliding door (which was apparently heard by Ischtar Akcan) and went over the empty loading area on the driver’s seat and went off. Thus, the statement that Ischtar Akcan observed the white box car and a man sitting at the seat of the driver is allowed to observe the Tathergang in the rear and saw how a man captured a girl and now felt that it was two different men, the one she saw sitting at the driver's seat by chance, and the one who actually did the act. After Natascha Kampusch claimed that at the time of the event only Wolfgang Priklopil was in the vehicle and only he did the deed and she was alone with him from the scene of the crime to the house in Heinestraße 60, Ischtar Akcan is expected to be wrong. Akcan Ischtar argued that it could have been as it was previously described by Colonel Kröll, and she would not doubt the particulars of the Natascha Kampusch. It is noted that the mother of Ischtar Akcan, Rosa Akcan, tried to influence her daughter during the conversation, but saw two perpetrators, and she could not imagine that her daughter would be wrong. It was explained by Colonel Kröll that an experience of this kind can indeed lead to irritations in observation and perception, and it is understandable that witnesses and victims who experience the terrible, due to the experience, information about and about a perpetrator or Tathergang, who then appear as not correctly perceived in the investigation of the perpetrator or clarification of the crime. Akcan Ischtar no longer acted on her daughter. As a result, Akcan Ischtar did not rule out the fact that the white boxing car perceived at the crossing of Rennbahnweg-Panethgasse could also have been a vehicle other than the vehicle. Outcome of the official act: 1. Kampusch Natascha, whose lawyer Dr. Ganzger, the caretaker of Natascha, Mr. Silveri, Akcan Ischtar and their mother Rosa Akcan, expressed a positive view of the conversation that came about, or the representation of the one experienced by Natascha Kampusch and Ischtar Akcan. 2. In particular, Rosa Akcan thanked Natascha Kampusch for the willingness to talk and argued to the officials that she felt relieved by the statements of Natascha Kampusch, especially since after only one perpetrator was involved and her daughter was also scared. 3rd. Akcan Ischtar claimed to the Cl Linzer that she could finally sleep quietly, especially since there was really only one perpetrator on March 02, 1998 and she obviously must have been wrong. She also claimed that she might have seen in the area of the Panethgasse a second boxing car that was not identical to the culprit vehicle. In any event, it is now easier for the facts that have been burdened on it for many years now. End of the official act: 03.12.2009, at 19:40.
